      "solution": "**Identify.** Closed-pipe resonance with tuning fork f = 320 Hz; successive resonance lengths L₁ = 20 cm, L₂ = 73 cm at 27 °C.\n\n**Concept.** Between two successive resonances the air-column length increases by λ/2 (end correction cancels).\n\n**Work.** λ/2 = L₂ − L₁ = 53 cm ⇒ λ = 106 cm = 1.06 m.
